# Step 03 — Source-to-Destination Migration Ledger

**Authority:** This is the live migration ledger. Step 01 inventories provide baseline locators and full sampletab wiring; this ledger records approved routes, staged-copy destinations, locator-level status, and follow-up decisions. “Baseline copied” is not “ported”: content review and final transfer decisions remain open for later numbered sessions.

**Step 04 evidence:** claim-by-claim sources and caveats for the Home/About rewrite are recorded in [`04-claim-evidence.md`](04-claim-evidence.md).

**Step 06 map:** Core Concepts section, anchor, capture, and sampletab decisions are recorded in [`06-core-concepts-transfer-map.md`](06-core-concepts-transfer-map.md).

**Step 07 map:** implementation evidence and cross-page context/target decisions are recorded in [`07-action-context-target-path-map.md`](07-action-context-target-path-map.md).

**Step 09 map:** technical review, section dispositions, and sampletab/capture checks for the four task guides are recorded in [`09-task-guides-transfer-map.md`](09-task-guides-transfer-map.md).

**Step 10 map:** practical/advanced topic dispositions, sample inventories, and source checks are recorded in [`10-practical-advanced-transfer-map.md`](10-practical-advanced-transfer-map.md).

**Step 11 map:** accessibility behavior, end-user copy, generated label associations, and the separate assistive-technology follow-up are recorded in [`11-accessibility-transfer-map.md`](11-accessibility-transfer-map.md) and [`11-accessibility-follow-up.md`](11-accessibility-follow-up.md).

**Step 12 map:** overview and Cheatsheet dispositions, type inventory, sample/capture transfers, and validation are recorded in [`12-component-reference-transfer-map.md`](12-component-reference-transfer-map.md).

**Step 13 map:** Form/List/Input source checks, preserved examples, verified defaults/actions, and follow-up notes are recorded in [`13-container-reference-transfer-map.md`](13-container-reference-transfer-map.md).

**Step 14 map:** scalar/control reference dispositions, source checks, sample/capture preservation, the Label test wiring, and current reset caveats are recorded in [`14-component-reference-scalar-and-interaction-types-transfer-map.md`](14-component-reference-scalar-and-interaction-types-transfer-map.md).

**Step 15 map:** File/Image/Video/Audio source checks, zero-byte/import distinctions, notices, preserved examples and capture wiring are recorded in [`15-media-types-transfer-map.md`](15-media-types-transfer-map.md).

**Step 16 map:** The Showcase candidate claims, all legacy sections/samples/captures/tests, maintainer decisions, and canonical destination coverage are recorded in [`16-showcase-audit-and-destination-decisions-transfer-map.md`](16-showcase-audit-and-destination-decisions-transfer-map.md).

**Step 17 map:** The finalized four-example Showcase, corrected claim/demo/test wiring, approved List/Mixin/Accessibility/Playground transfers, and current capture inventory are recorded in [`17-final-showcase-transfer-map.md`](17-final-showcase-transfer-map.md).

## Support and excluded sources

- `docs/_resources/examples.capture.md` remains shared source infrastructure. Identical same-directory support copies are mapped at `/old/resources/examples.capture` and `/showcase_examples/examples.capture`; the capture data is not a public chapter or a reader-facing transfer.
- Nine standalone downloadable Pug pages, their CSV rows, templates, CSS, and controller captures remain under `src/examples/` and `docs/_data/examples.csv`; Step 03 changes only the consumer/build plumbing. Step 18 owns any content redistribution decision.
- `docs/_community/contact.md` is byte-for-byte unchanged at `/community/contact`; the no-edit constraint is not waived by this ledger.
- New pages (Using SmarkForm, When SmarkForm Fits, Accessible Labels and Names) are explicit placeholders, not ports. Their source content is assigned by later steps.
- Step 17 retargeted root `README.md` feature links to the current Showcase example anchors or the relevant List, Accessibility, Masking, Animations, Playground, and home-page destinations; its school data demo links to the new deep List sample. The FAQ's animation/list example links were also retargeted.
- `migration_in_progress`, `migration_legacy`, `migration_stub`, and `sitemap: false` are temporary controls; remove them with all `_old_*` collections and migration comments before publication.

## Completion rule

Update individual section/include/capture rows only after checking the actual canonical destination and preserving the full example association. For transfers that split or merge, list every source and destination in the affected row and add/adjust paired source/destination comments. Record deliberate non-ports with rationale. Do not mark page rows complete merely because their baseline copies are present.
